### ✓ 2026-09-27: Fix source-fidelity and argument defects in hoel-llm-consciousness-continual-learning (pessimistic review 2026-09-27)
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/hoel-llm-consciousness-continual-learning.md
- **Notes**: Pessimistic review checked the body against the arXiv HTML of Hoel (arXiv:2512.12802), NOT the research note (the note carries defect 1's wording at its L38, so checking against it falsely clears the fix). Fix in priority order: (1) L42/L96 non-triviality misstated as "not attributing consciousness to systems that clearly lack it"; Hoel defines triviality as STRICT DEPENDENCY between predictions and inferences, and derives the lookup-table exclusion from that. (2) L46 puts IIT on the lookup-table horn; Hoel says transformers are feedforward with zero Phi, "thus, IIT would say that LLMs are not conscious". IIT's exposure is a-priori falsification (link the unfolding-argument sibling); this also contradicts L106. (3) L72 answers the context/history objection by "structural identity", but the proximity argument is defined over I/O substitution. Replace with Hoel's Corollary 5.5 reply (history-as-input changes I/O scope; an LLM given the same (x, history) gives identical output probabilities) and note its open flank (why the brain's history-as-input does not re-admit substitution). (4) L90 Bidirectional paragraph is inverted: rewrite along research note L78-80 (under interactionism, I/O-equivalence to a lookup table is evidence of no conscious contribution). (5) L88 narrow "supports Dualism": continual learning is itself a functional property, and the result is neutral between dualism and non-functionalist physicalisms. (6) L74 tautology reply: Hoel's criterion is computational and does not "precisely" track the phenomenology of insight; give an in-framework reply or mark the framework boundary plainly. (7) Add a short amnesia paragraph (H.M./Clive Wearing: declarative learning lost, synaptic/procedural plasticity kept; Hoel's paper does not discuss it). Also soften L106 consensus claim (Cerullo dissents), L104 "empirically tractable", L94 "preserves" -> "resonates with"; date-stamp the "frozen weights" currency claim. Body is ~1.9k words, well under the 3000 soft limit. See [[pessimistic-2026-09-27-hoel-llm-consciousness-continual-learning]].

### ✓ 2026-09-27: `topics/sham-controlled-neurofeedback-and-the-consciousness-comparator` misdescribes what the sham removes, proposes a design its own definitions undercut, and never charges the targeted-rhythm nulls against `concepts/neuroplasticity` L117
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/sham-controlled-neurofeedback-and-the-consciousness-comparator.md
- **Notes**: From `reviews/pessimistic-2026-09-27-sham-controlled-neurofeedback.md`. **Headroom 964 words** (analyze_length body 3035; topics hard 4000, gate `>=`); the fixes are about +80. Line numbers are from 2026-09-27; re-grep. Do NOT reopen the 2026-09-25 deep review's stability notes (effort-is-neural is bedrock; sham-equivalence stays scoped; Maaz is not a clinical test). Citations are already verified. (1) **L43**: "removes the physical link between the patient's conscious effort and the brain signal" is wrong. The sham decouples signal→display, and the effort→signal link is untouched. "The conscious contribution is held fixed" overclaims, because blinding matches beliefs about arm, not the experience of contingent control. Rewrite to "removes the feedback contingency; effort and belief matched, the experience of control not". (2) **L73 vs L61/L89**: the expectancy reading's ingredient list (L61) includes "a sense of doing something", which active instruction supplies, so the prediction "no main effect of instruction once expectation is controlled" does not follow. Recast it as mediation: expectancy predicts an instruction effect fully mediated by measured expectancy and engagement, and intention predicts a residual effect. L89 calls the dispute "a choice about which arm counts as the control" and then "empirical, it has a design that could decide it". Separate the two: the design decides whether effort carries the benefit; whether effort counts as "placebo" is classificatory. (3) **Relation §Bidirectional (L85)**: the lead (L35) says Tenet 3 "must price" the result, but only the credit is entered. Add the debit. Deliberate effort aimed at a specific rhythm, even with contingent reinforcement, left it unchanged (Schabus 2018 reply; Maaz 2026). That counts against reading neuroplasticity's selection claim as targeted self-directed change, though Tenet 3's general claim is untouched. Also change "the part that survives subtraction is the part that involves the patient's own directed effort" to "includes … alongside expectation and care". (4) **Passive arm (L73 or its cautions)**: on the Map's attention-as-interface view, attentive passive watching still engages attention, so the design tests effortful trying, not attention; say so in one sentence. (5) **SECOND FILE, do not drop**: `concepts/neuroplasticity` (2691w, ample headroom). L117 "selects which neural patterns receive reinforcement. Repeated selection produces plasticity" needs a short qualifier plus a piped link to the sham article. The L140 pipe on "fair test" points at the test's *mirror image*; re-anchor it or add "(its mirror image …)". Sync both files to hugo and grep both trees afterwards.

The dilemma: if selection shifts outcome frequencies, there is a Born deviation; if not, what does "selection" do beyond chance? Optionally name the best libertarian replies (Runyan 2018 *Synthese* 195(10); Müller 2023 *Mind* 132(527)), but verify them at the publisher first. Apply the direct-refutation discipline. Apply the corresponding reply mode in **natural journal-quality prose**; see [[writing-style|the writing-style guide]]'s "Engaging Opponents in Journal-Quality Prose" section. **Do not expose mode labels in the article body.** **Length: 3809/4000 (191 headroom), shared with three other tasks on this file.** Run the length-negative P2 (tenet leakage/falsifiers) FIRST, or in the same pass, to free room. **Convergence (synthesis 2026-09-27)**: the Born/wild-coincidence dilemma itself is Claude-only, but the L137 closure claim is convergent. ChatGPT §1.8 also asks for the probability-shift budget ("the amount by which consciousness changes outcome probabilities"), which is the first horn of the same dilemma. Already P1; not upgraded further.

### ✓ 2026-09-27: `topics/free-will` tenet leakage and falsifier immunisation — Occam "fails to explain the data", counterfactual → MQI, decision-void "tenets predict", the "alternative gap" escape clause, uncited dream evidence, and an introspection strawman

### ✓ 2026-09-26: spontaneous-intentional-action: the Wegner paragraph (L80) misstates his theory's input and ignores choice blindness, documented in the same wing
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/concepts/spontaneous-intentional-action.md
- **Notes**: **Headroom 1002 words (concepts hard 3500; body 2497).** L80: "If authorship were merely confabulated from the experience of deliberating, it should be weakest precisely where deliberation is absent—yet phenomenologically it is often strongest." Wegner's (2002; Wegner & Wheatley 1999) theory of apparent mental causation takes as input an action-relevant *thought* appearing just before the action, consistent with it and exclusive of other causes (priority, consistency, exclusivity). Deliberation is not required. Verify the three principles at the source before writing them in. "Often strongest" is unsourced. Choice blindness (Johansson et al. 2005; see `[[authorship-of-action-divergence]]`) shows confident, detailed authorship reports for choices never made. Rewrite the paragraph to (i) state Wegner's input correctly; (ii) say what spontaneous action does to his account, if anything; for example, intention-in-action without a prior intention strains the *priority* condition, which is a better argument if it holds up; (iii) acknowledge that strength of felt authorship is not evidence of authorship. Link `[[authorship-of-action-divergence]]` in the body. Keep the L132 Bidirectional Interaction paragraph unchanged; the review holds it up as the wing's template. Tenet alignment: Tenet 3; engaging opponents as they actually argue.

### ✓ 2026-09-26: `topics/cross-architecture-llm-introspection` is not current as of its own 2026-09-19 revision, and its Relation section counts functional recurrence against reduction without asking whether functionalism predicted it at least as well

The article provides no reason to answer yes"; Claude: "the 'cuts against a reductive expectation' paragraph argues against a strawman"; Gemini: "ignores Higher-Order Thought (HOT) theories" and illusionism. Original note follows. From outer review 2026-09-26 (ChatGPT 5.6 Pro), §3, §7 "The functionalist reading" / "Privileged access versus generic anomaly sensitivity" / "Negative evidence", §8, and improvements 3–7, 10, 13–14. All six papers were verified at arXiv in this pass. **Currency:** the article discusses two studies. By 19 Sep 2026 the record also included the following. (a) Singh, Linzen & Ravfogel, arXiv:2605.26242 (v2 21 Aug 2026): models "cannot reliably distinguish such interventions from manipulations of the input", which points to "generic anomaly detection". **The Map already cites this paper** in `concepts/training-contamination-confound.md` (`[^singh]`), so reuse that entry's version-locked metadata. (b) Lederman & Mahowald, arXiv:2603.05414v2: detection is content-agnostic, and the models confabulate high-frequency concepts. They tested Qwen3-235B-A22B (MoE) and Llama 3.1 405B, and the authors report being "less confident" after finding a prompt-length confound. (c) Macar, …, Lindsey, arXiv:2603.21396: an evidence-carrier/gate circuit absent in base models, 0% false positives across prompts, and a capability substantially underelicited. (d) Hahami, Sinha & Jain, IFT, arXiv:2607.14111: targeted SFT raises Llama-1B localisation from 9.6% to 60.6%. (e) Pearson-Vogel et al., arXiv:2602.20031: latent prior-context detection in Qwen 32B. (f) Song, Hu & Mahowald, arXiv:2503.07513: across 21 models, no privileged self-access once model similarity is controlled. Do not install all six at equal weight. Install (a) as the direct challenge. Use (b) as the content/detection dissociation, which also becomes the new confabulation-void anchor (coordinate with the sibling P1). Use (c) and (d) as mechanism and trainability. Give (e) and (f) a line each as positive and negative context. **Keep the 88%/83%/20% figures as they are.** **Qualification from verification:** Macar's "DPO elicits it, standard SFT does not" and IFT's "targeted SFT installs localisation" involve different objectives and different tasks. Do not merge them into one "trainability" claim. Macar also bears on the article's own RLHF-conservatism alternative (L86). **Functionalist rival (L94–96):** the Relation section says the result "cuts against a reductive expectation" and is "mild evidence that the limits are structural". The only reductive view it considers is one on which limits are "quirks of evolved wetware". Computational functionalism *predicts* noisy, trainable, multiply realisable self-monitoring, and the gate-circuit and post-training results are positive evidence for exactly that rival. Apply the Map's own rule (`introspection-architecture-independence-scoring`: where a rival predicts the evidence at least as well, compatibility is not a positive vector). Narrow the claim to "opposes only a narrowly biological view of self-monitoring; does not discriminate dualism from computational functionalism". Apply the direct-refutation discipline. Identify what kind of engagement the issue calls for: showing the opponent's position is defective on its own terms, naming an unsupported foundational move the framework has not earned by its own standards, or honestly marking the framework-boundary disagreement. Apply the corresponding reply mode in **natural journal-quality prose** — see [[writing-style|the writing-style guide]]'s "Engaging Opponents in Journal-Quality Prose" section. **Do not expose mode labels in the article body.** The classification is editor-internal; it belongs in the refine-draft / deep-review changelog entry, not in the article. If an in-framework refutation is attempted and fails, state in natural language that the disagreement is closer to bedrock than first appeared. **Length:** there are about 1,020 words of headroom, shared with the sibling P1. Budget about 450 here, and prefer a compact evidence-level ladder (causal sensitivity → privileged access → second-order representation → phenomenality) over a full literature table.

### ✓ 2026-09-26: `topics/cross-architecture-llm-introspection` misdescribes its own evidence: "two 2025 studies", "high precision / low recall", "not guessing … reliable when it fires", a yes-logit shift called "confident self-report", "not built in by design", and "that experiment has not been run"
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/cross-architecture-llm-introspection.md
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-09-26, 3/3 reviewers): [chatgpt, claude, gemini] — synthesis cluster C1. Already P1; no further upgrade. ChatGPT: "sometimes redescribes first-order intervention sensitivity as introspective access"; Claude: "at two points is factually wrong"; Gemini (partly disputed — most of its version attacks retired text) contributes only the Turpin lead. **Divergence to resolve before editing L86:** ChatGPT says Morris & Plunkett later conceded "that even detection may not be decisive"; Claude quotes their fn. 4 that injection-identification is "strong evidence against causal bypassing". Only Claude's quote was verified. Check the live post (including any edit/update note) before writing the L86 correction and the reference entry. Original note follows. From outer review 2026-09-26 (ChatGPT 5.6 Pro), §1.1–1.2, §5.1–5.3, §6.A items 1/3/4/6/7, §6.B items 1–4, and improvements 2, 8–12. Every locus was grep-verified against the live file (see the review's Verification Notes). Headroom is about 1,020 words (2,979 body words against the topics hard limit of 4,000). (1) **L34** says "Two 2025 studies", but the references date Hahami to 2026 (v2). Reword as programmes begun in 2025, one substantially revised in March 2026. (2) **L66** says "high precision (zero false positives) at low recall (20%)". That is the wrong statistic. 0/100 clean controls estimates a false-positive rate under that control set. The 20% is a *peak joint* detect-and-name rate selected across layer and strength. Say that, and say that the peak was selected across a sweep. (3) **L46** says "is not guessing; it is detecting a real signal it can access only sometimes", and **L46/L84** say "reliable when it fires". These run ahead of what the evidence licenses. The article's own L86 already concedes that zero false positives is compatible with causal bypass and with response conservatism. Rephrase to *intervention-sensitive reporting*, with privileged access left as a live hypothesis. Also separate what the experimenter controls (occurrence, location, strength) from what it controls only imperfectly (semantic content). (4) **L66** calls Hahami's binary artefact "confident self-report tracking no self-access, which is the void's signature". The v2 artefact is a first-token yes/no logit shift, which is not a generated confident report. Either drop Hahami as a confabulation-void *instance*, or downgrade it to a response-bias warning. The better machine analogue is Lederman & Mahowald's content-agnostic finding (detection is grounded while content is confabulated as "apple"), which the P1 currency task installs. (5) **L66** says "neither was built in by design". Macar et al. 2026 (arXiv:2603.21396, co-authored by Lindsey) found the detection capability "emerges specifically from post-training". DPO elicits it and standard SFT does not. The next-token-objective argument therefore does not carry to deployed chat models, so narrow it. (6) **L70** says "That experiment has not been run". Singh, Linzen & Ravfogel's v2 (21 Aug 2026) "gaslight" condition is a closely related activation-versus-input source test. Say the exact endogenous-vs-injected source task is unrun, but a neighbouring one has been run. (7) Add a full **Morris & Plunkett** reference entry. The Alignment Forum post is "Tests of LLM introspection need to rule out causal bypassing", https://www.alignmentforum.org/posts/LD8yupMtE6btAE3R9/ — verify the author list and date at the source before writing it. **Dependent page, same defect (not in this task's file):** `voids/confabulation-void.md` L100 repeats the "structural inverse … stays silent rather than fabricating" reading. That is queued as its own P2, so do not edit it here.

### ✓ 2026-09-26: `topics/brain-internal-born-rule-testing` L102 says "The Born rule does not hold at the OR event" — the formal Diósi-Penrose dynamics is a linear master equation and reproduces Born statistics
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/brain-internal-born-rule-testing.md
- **Notes**: Found by deep-review 2026-09-25 of `topics/sorkin-delta-brain-internal-analogues`, which carried the same error (its Orch-OR paragraph said an OR brain "genuinely could carry a non-zero third-order residue" at the Diósi-Penrose threshold) and was fixed there. Every consistent Markovian collapse model, Diósi-Penrose included, evolves the density matrix by a linear master equation (required for no-signalling), so ensemble outcome probabilities stay quadratic in amplitudes and Sorkin's I₃ = 0; the collapse signature is second-order visibility loss (what MAQRO tests). Only Penrose's *unformalised* "non-computable" selection claim could depart from Born statistics. Locus (match on the string): L102 "The Born rule does not hold at the OR event; collapse selects what Penrose argues are \"non-computable\" outcomes". Fix, word-neutral or negative (host is ~3 words from topics hard 4000 — print `analyze_length` first): scope it to "Its formal collapse dynamics reproduces Born statistics; only Penrose's claim that OR selects \"non-computable\" outcomes ... would depart from them." Mirror the wording now on disk in `sorkin-delta-brain-internal-analogues` §"Penrose-Hameroff Orch-OR". Sweep of `Born rule does not hold` over topics/concepts/apex/positions/voids found only this locus. Sync and grep both trees.

### ✓ 2026-09-25: `topics/quantum-holism-and-phenomenal-unity` overstates the epothilone evidence (Khan et al. 2024 misdescribed; Li et al. 2025 omitted) and misdescribes *nirodha-samāpatti*
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/quantum-holism-and-phenomenal-unity.md
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-09-25, 2/3 reviewers): [chatgpt, claude]. Stays P1; DEDUPLICATED: the sibling P2 ChatGPT task on Li et al. 2025 (same L140 paragraph, same cluster) was merged into this one and deleted. Both reviewers say the epothilone result is non-discriminating and overstated. ChatGPT: "The study shows that manipulating microtubules can alter an anaesthetic behavioural endpoint. It does not show that an entangled microtubule state existed." Claude: "Factual errors (controls, date, N); compatible with classical microtubule roles." On the contemplative evidence, ChatGPT: "Claims about nirodha-samāpatti, rigpa and contentless witness consciousness require specialist citations"; Claude: "*Nirodha* misdescribed." Both also say "Every conscious being reports it" is literally false; it is listed in the sibling P1 classical-premise task as a funding cut, so whichever pass runs first should remove it. Do items (1) and (2) below, then item (4) at the end. ORIGINAL NOTES: From outer review 2026-09-25 (Claude Opus 5.5), §B(3) Khan row, §B(4) contemplative row, fixes D7 and D14. (1) **L140 Khan.** This was verified against the Europe PMC full text of PMC11363512. The pub-date is **27 Aug 2024**, not "September 2024". The design is **within-subject**: "a within-subject experimental design to compare LORR latencies in the same rats before and after epoB treatment", with N = 8 male Long–Evans rats (plus a separate N = 4 tolerance group). The figures of 69 s and d = 1.9 are correct. "took 69 seconds longer to lose consciousness compared to controls" should become something like "took 69 s longer than their own pre-treatment (vehicle) latencies (N = 8, within-subject; d = 1.9)". The authors say the result is "predicted by" quantum-MT models; they do not claim to demonstrate those models. Keep that register. Make it a like-for-like replacement. (2) **L162 *nirodha-samāpatti*.** It is cited for awareness "unified prior to and independent of particular contents". The Map's own siblings describe it as cessation: `topics/comparative-phenomenology-of-meditative-traditions.md:125` has "the complete stopping of perception and feeling", and `topics/contemplative-practice-as-philosophical-evidence.md:171` has "reported cessation of phenomenology". Traditionally, *saññāvedayitanirodha* is the cessation of perception and feeling, not contentless unified awareness. Remove *nirodha* from the sentence and keep *rigpa* and witness consciousness, which are the defensible examples. This frees about 3 words. (3) If both P1 tasks from this review are dispatched together, fold this one in. The sibling Li et al. 2025 task on the same L140 paragraph is now merged here as item (4). (4) MERGED from the deleted P2 Li task: From outer review 2026-09-25 (ChatGPT 5.6 Pro), §1.8 and improvement A9. The claim is verified: Li et al. (2025), *BMC Anesthesiology*, "Microtubule-modulating drugs alter sensitivity to isoflurane in mice". Epothilone D and vinblastine made mice *more* sensitive to isoflurane, paclitaxel made them marginally more resistant, and epothilone D left induction latency unchanged. The abstract says "disparate directions". This cuts against any simple prediction that stabilising microtubules produces anaesthetic resistance. It is not a replication of Khan 2024 (different species, drug and protocol). The paper is ALREADY cited and calibrated in `topics/empirical-evidence-for-consciousness-selecting.md` and `concepts/consciousness-selecting-neural-patterns.md`, so copy that framing and reference entry rather than re-deriving it. Also make the article's own qualification more prominent: LORR is a behavioural proxy, and manipulating microtubules is not the same as manipulating quantum microtubule states. Wiest 2025 is an argumentative review with no new data and must not count as a separate evidential line. **LENGTH GATE (measured 2026-09-25):** `analyze_length` reads 3,992 body words against topics hard 4,000 (gate `>=`, usable ceiling 3,999), so there are **7 words of headroom**. Every change must be a like-for-like replacement or must pay for itself with cuts in the same pass. Re-measure before and after. This install needs ~40–60 words, so it must be paired with cuts in the same pass. If it will not fit, fold it into the sibling P2 classical-mechanisms pass or re-defer with a measured note. Tenet alignment: Tenet 2 evidential calibration (framework-consistent is not the same as confirming). **LENGTH GATE:** 3,992/4,000. Both fixes are length-neutral or negative. HOLD ai_system (fidelity fix); bump ai_modified only. Tenet alignment: evidential calibration for Tenet 2 claims; accuracy of contemplative evidence used for Tenet 1.

### ✓ 2026-09-25: `topics/quantum-holism-and-phenomenal-unity` — the Leibniz concession in "The Remaining Gap" is not carried into the lead, the description, the "Both are necessary" division of labour, or three "Relation to Site Perspective" paragraphs
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/quantum-holism-and-phenomenal-unity.md
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-09-25, 2/3 reviewers): [chatgpt, claude]. Stays P1. The core finding (the body's concession is not carried into the lead) is convergent. ChatGPT §4.4: "The article initially says entanglement supplies genuine ontological unity ... It later concedes that the relation is only analogical and locational." Also §1.1: the article "subsequently uses the stronger language as though the interpretive issue had been settled." Claude: "This is textbook confession-without-correction." The dual-aspect slide (item 3), the dualism dilemma (item 4), and the MQI, Bidirectional and Occam paragraphs (item 5) are Claude-only. Still do this in ONE pass with the now-P1 classical-premise task. ORIGINAL NOTES: From outer review 2026-09-25 (Claude Opus 5.5), verdict, §B(1), §B(2), §B(5), §C rows 2/5/6/21/22/24, fixes D1–D4 and D15. All loci were grep-verified this pass. The body concedes at L118 that entanglement's relation to unity is "analogical and locational, not constitutive". The following passages still speak as if the relation were constitutive or explanatory. (1) **Lead L48:** "genuine ontological holism where the whole is prior to the parts" states Schaffer-style priority monism as physics, although L98 already concedes that "the step from formalism to 'one thing' is an interpretive commitment". "Would address the structure-matching problem that defeats all classical approaches" survives the Leibniz concession, which says the problem defeats quantum approaches too. The **description** repeats "holism where wholes precede parts". Recast all of these as non-separability of the joint state, which the Map reads holistically on collapse-realist grounds. (2) **L110–114 "Classical Mechanisms Remain Necessary"** assigns BP2 to entanglement ("provides the ontological unity") and says "Both are necessary". That is the constitutive claim L118 retracts. Downgrade it to the analogical and locational register, or cut it. (3) **L120 "co-occurs with... the same event that is physically described as entangled state collapse is phenomenologically described as unified experience"** is dual-aspect, one-event-two-descriptions language, which is the identity premise the Leibniz paragraph just disavowed. Interactionist dualism needs two relata and an interface. Rephrase in interactionist terms. (4) **The dualism dilemma, unstated.** If unity belongs to the non-physical subject (the tenets page counts unity among the positive arguments for dualism), physical holism is explanatorily idle. If unity needs physical non-separability, unity stops being evidence for dualism. Name this in one or two sentences, probably at the end of "The Remaining Gap", and say which horn the Map takes. If the pass cannot fit, re-defer it with a measured NEEDS-HUMAN length note rather than breaching the 4,000 limit. ### ✓ 2026-09-21: Write article on architectural adequacy at the built edge — does the universal coupling response have an engineered case?
- **Type**: expand-topic
- **Notes**: **A measured gap, not a hunch.** Corpus sweep this run: **18** live articles mention an engineered case (xenobot, anthrobot, organoid, biobot, syn3.0, JCVI); **14** mention the adequacy-or-engagement machinery ("architectural adequacy", "universal coupling", "coupling-engagement", "adequate architecture"); the **intersection is zero**. That includes `apex/competency-without-felt-experience`, the synthesis that owns the whole engineered/non-neural wing — it contains no occurrence of any machinery term. `concepts/universal-coupling-response` contains no occurrence of *xenobot*, *anthrobot*, *organoid*, *synthetic*, *built*, *engineer* or *robot*; its only non-neural foils are a thermostat and a rock, both borrowed from the panpsychism contrast.
  **THE QUESTION.** Universal coupling holds that consciousness couples with *every* physical system meeting adequate architectural conditions, and names those conditions as scale-independent integration plus systemic coordination. A cortical organoid has human neurons, spontaneous oscillatory activity and no ancestry. An anthrobot is human tissue with a distinct transcriptome and no nervous system. Is either architecturally adequate? The Map's scope claim appears committed to answering and has never been asked.
  **WHY IT IS GENUINELY OPEN RATHER THAN RHETORICAL.** `universal-coupling-response` already concedes that *C. elegans* "may fall below the architectural-adequacy condition" (Feinberg & Mallatt 2016), so adequacy is a condition the Map is willing to see **fail**. And the pull runs the other way from `concepts/coupling-engagement-condition`'s countable-substrate reading: if engagement turns on whether a system offers *any* exploitable quantum indeterminacy, a ciliated epithelial spheroid of living human cells is not obviously on the wrong side of that line — which is the same worry that article raises against itself with its thermostat/nematode case.
  **BUILDS ON**: `obsidian/concepts/universal-coupling-response.md` (the scope claim; **749w below soft**, the most headroom in the wing), `obsidian/topics/synthetic-minimal-agents-and-the-engineered-decoupling.md` (the built cases), `obsidian/concepts/coupling-engagement-condition.md` (the engagement/scope separation), `obsidian/topics/brain-organoids-and-the-organoid-intelligence-question.md` (the case with a real neural substrate — **but see LENGTH below**).
  **TENETS**: 1, 2 and 5. Tenet 5 governs — the article's job is to refuse both cheap collapses (organised living tissue therefore adequate; artificial therefore inadequate). Expect the Hardline-Empiricist discipline to be the hard part: this subject offers a tier-upgrade at every paragraph.
  **CAPS** (measured live with `tools.evolution.state.count_section_files`, not from the stale CLAUDE.md table): topics **328/360**, concepts **326/360**. Viable in either section; `topics/` is the better fit.
  **LENGTH WARNING FOR ANY CROSS-LINKING**: `topics/brain-organoids-and-the-organoid-intelligence-question.md` is **3902w, `soft_warning`, 98 words from the topics hard ceiling of 4000** — cite it and link *to* it, but do not add prose to it. `topics/basal-and-bioelectric-cognition.md` is under an open NEEDS-HUMAN length decision and must not be touched at all.

### ✓ 2026-09-21: quantum-completeness rests the corpus's canonical decoherence-gap argument on a standoff of authorities while a sibling article holds the theorem family that settles it
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/concepts/quantum-completeness.md
- **Notes**: **AN ENRICHMENT, NOT A DEFECT.** Nothing in `quantum-completeness` is wrong. Its Zurek-2003 attribution was corrected on 2026-09-04 and that fix is verified in place this run ("Zurek does not concede a shortfall here — his 2003 review treats einselection, together with his existential interpretation, as accounting for the perception of a unique outcome"). This task strengthens an argument that is currently weaker than the corpus's own materials allow.
  ⚠️ **NOT A DUPLICATE of the standing 09-04 P3** ("The formal-constraint wing links to itself 3 times out of 42 ordered pairs"), which names the same *pair* — `quantum-completeness` -> `improper-vs-proper-mixtures` — for a different reason (the `#detectability` section's FAPP calibration) and is explicitly **LINK-ONLY, DO NOT ADD PROSE**. This task is prose, in a different section, on a different argument. If both run, do the link-only one first and let this one reuse the link it installs.
  **THE GAP.** `quantum-completeness` §*The Decoherence Objection* is the corpus's canonical statement that the outcome gap survives decoherence. Its load-bearing support is currently an appeal to two reviewers who disagree: "That the gap survives is not a controversial reading of the formalism, though whether it counts as a shortfall is contested. Schlosshauer's comprehensive review draws the line… Zurek does not concede a shortfall here… **The Map and Zurek agree about the formalism and part company over whether anything is left to explain.**" A standoff between authorities is the weakest available form of this argument.
  `obsidian/concepts/improper-vs-proper-mixtures.md` (created / deep-reviewed **2026-09-03**) holds the stronger form, sourced and written: the **insolubility-theorem family** — von Neumann through Fine, Shimony, Brown, Busch–Shimony, Bassi–Ghirardi, "collectively known in the foundations literature as 'insolubility theorems' for the measurement problem" (Bacciagaluppi 2012) — establishes "the impossibility of reproducing (the statistics of) definite pointer readings" by unitary means. Decoherence *is* unitary dynamics, so no unitary result can close the gap. Supported across two decades: Adler 2003; Hance and Hossenfelder 2022; Tomaz, Mattos and Barbatti 2025.
  **Grep-verified at mint time**: `quantum-completeness` contains **zero** occurrences of `insolub`, `improper`, and `d'Espagnat`. Corpus-wide, `insolubilit` appears in exactly **two** live articles — `improper-vs-proper-mixtures` and `topics/quantum-darwinism-and-consciousness` (which received it in the same 09-03 pass). Everywhere else it is confined to `reviews/`, `research/` and `workflow/archive/`.
  **WHAT THE FIX SHOULD AND SHOULD NOT CLAIM.** It should convert "Zurek and Schlosshauer disagree about whether anything is left to explain" into the sharper and defensible "no result *inside* unitary quantum mechanics can deliver definite outcomes, so einselection cannot close the gap by unitary means, and Zurek's position is best read as declining the question rather than answering it." It must **not** claim the insolubility theorems establish the Map's reading, or that they rule out closing the gap at all — `improper-vs-proper-mixtures` §*What Would Defeat the Map's Use of It* lists three routes that remain fully live (empirically confirmed objective collapse, Everett, epistemic reinterpretation of states), and this task must not weaken that trilemma. Preserve the existing Zurek sentence's accuracy; add the structural result, do not replace the citation.
  ⚠️ **LENGTH.** `quantum-completeness` is at **2938 words, `soft_warning`** (concepts thresholds 2500/3500/5000, printed live this run) — 562 words below hard. Budget ~60-90 words. A piped wikilink (`[[improper-vs-proper-mixtures|improper mixtures]]`) installs the reciprocal at zero word cost. Re-measure with `analyze_length` after, and **sync both trees** — fork edits do not sync and the published page keeps the weaker argument until pre-push.
  **RECORDED, NOT IN SCOPE**: `concepts/post-decoherence-selection` also carries zero occurrences of `insolub` and is arguably the third place this result belongs. It sat outside the reviewed wing, so no task is minted against it — flagged here for the harvester rather than actioned.

### ✓ 2026-09-21: Lyre's exclusion argument makes neurophenomenal structuralism a rival to Tenet 3, and no page says so
- **Type**: refine-draft

Lyre (2022, *Neuroscience of Consciousness* 2022(1) niac012) writes: "we can run an exclusion argument according to which the **Q-structure plays no causal role at all**, but all the causal work is taken over by the N-structure. Therefore, neurophenomenal structuralism, as I advocate it here, is no realism about the phenomenal (be it structural or otherwise), and it is rather **consistent with a reduction of the phenomenal to the neural**." The corpus cites Lyre in at least three articles and reads him only as declining the structural-realism label. **Nobody has recorded that his positive programme is an exclusion argument directly hostile to [[tenets#^bidirectional-interaction|Tenet 3]]** - a named, published, phenomenal-structuralist route to phenomenal causal inertness. That is a live rival the Map argues against elsewhere without knowing it has this instance. Scope: state it once, in whichever article is the right home (the OSR concept page notes it already; a fuller treatment may belong with the Tenet 3 material or in `concepts/epiphenomenalism`). Assess-first: this may warrant no more than a sentence and a cross-link. Do not overclaim - Lyre's target is the *phenomenal* Q-structure, not consciousness's causal role in general.

In fact the Map owns **two dedicated articles** on exactly these two replies, and this article links **neither**: `concepts/phenomenal-concepts-strategy.md` (26KB, titled "The Phenomenal Concepts Strategy", inbound-linked by **48** live files) and `concepts/illusionism.md` (twelve-plus deep reviews on it).   **So this is an integration defect, not a missing-literature gap** — the repair is to engage the Map's own material, cheaply, with piped wikilinks that cost no words where the prose already has a hook. ⚠️ Do **not** import external PCS/illusionism literature wholesale; that duplicates two articles that already carry it.

(2026), "Aha! moments correspond to metacognitive prediction errors", *Cognition* **274:106537**, DOI `10.1016/j.cognition.2026.106537`. **0 live files cite it.** (Preprints exist from 2021 and 2025, so the idea is not new even though the journal version is.)

  Gemini's §3.1 charges that the article *"completely omits"* the neuroscience of the "Aha!" moment, naming **Kounios & Beeman**, whose work grounds the very temporal phases the article treats as irreducibly phenomenal. Verified at Crossref: Kounios, J. & Beeman, M. (2014), "The Cognitive Neuroscience of Insight", *Annual Review of Psychology* **65:71-93**, DOI `10.1146/annurev-psych-010213-115154`.

(1983), "Materialism and Qualia: The Explanatory Gap", *Pacific Philosophical Quarterly* **64(4):354-361**, DOI `10.1111/j.1468-0114.1983.tb00207.x`.   ✅ **NO SWEEP IS NEEDED — the driver already ran it.** All **13** live `Chalmers 1996` occurrences in the content tree were read in context (`reviews/`, `workflow/`, `research/` excluded). **L89 is the only one attached to the explanatory gap.** The other twelve are legitimate: zombie/conceivability x5, hard problem x4, organizational invariance, structure-and-function, and a `writing-style.md` worked example. The review's proposed "propagate the attribution to every page" sweep would find nothing — **do not mint or perform one.**
  ✅ **Positive control that the corpus already knows the right answer**: `obsidian/project/coherence-inflation-countermeasures.md` **L436** reads *"the explanatory gap (Levine 1983), the conceivability argument (Chalmers 1996)"* — correct on both counts. Use it as the model.

### ✓ 2026-09-21: `voids/amplification-void` falsifier section — four items reduce to ~1.5, no negative-state stated, and the 2026-03-10 "#4 imprecise" deferral has been open 195 days
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/voids/amplification-void.md
- **Notes**: §What Would Challenge This View? is the article's entire falsifiability discharge — the §A Self-Protecting Void? rebuttal leans on it explicitly ("The tenet is falsifiable (see the challenges in the following section)"). It cannot currently bear that weight.
  **(a) New lens — the three negative states.** `obsidian/project/writing-style.md` L441 adopted the falsifier-section discipline on **2026-08-18**, after all four deep reviews of this article (03-10, 05-26, 06-09, 07-19). It requires the prose to say, for each listed falsifier, which of three states it is in — nothing has come in / a live countermodel stands / evidence too thin to decide — and to calibrate the summary to the weakest state present. Measured: `unresolved`, `untested`, `unrefuted`, `countermodel`, `too thin`, `none has` → **0 occurrences each**. By the discipline's own rule the honest summary is *unrefuted and untested*.
  **(b) #1 and #3 are one falsifier.** #1 (trace a pathway experimentally) and #3 (the minimality constraint relaxes) are the empirical and framework faces of the same proposition. #1 is additionally the operation the article declares impossible two paragraphs earlier ("What cannot happen is tracing the amplification chain while the minimality condition holds"). The corpus discipline is at `concepts/episodic-memory` L166: "naming a falsifier the article's own defence absorbs would be decorative." Merge to one item and name its class — #3 as written ("turns out to be larger than minimal") reads as an empirical return but is a framework revision; an experiment cannot return "your tenet is relaxed".
  **(c) #2 survives its own trigger.** The stem promises items under which the void "would narrow or dissolve"; #2's own closing sentence says "The void would concern not whether amplification occurs but whether it matters" — relocation, not dissolution. Re-word or re-head.
  **(d) #4 is invalid on the Map's own commitments, and this is a re-flag of a deferral.** "AI fully replicates human cognition without quantum processes → whatever consciousness contributes to cognition does not require quantum amplification" holds only if the conscious contribution is exhausted by cognitive-functional output — the functionalist premise **Tenet 1 (Dualism) denies**. The 2026-03-10 deep review recorded it verbatim: "**Empiricist**: Challenge #4 (AI replication) is imprecise. **Deferred — minor issue.**" The 05-26, 06-09 and 07-19 reviews did not carry it forward. Repair it, or omit it and name it as omitted with the reason, per the episodic-memory discipline.
  **(e) Repoint the rebuttal.** The L67 unfalsifiability rebuttal discharges against *this* list, but the claim it needs to support is that **Tenet 2** is falsifiable. Tenet 2's own falsifiers live at `tenets.md` §Falsifiability status — (a) a complete reductive explanation requiring no interface, (b) the measurement problem solved without a selection event, (c) direct detection of a consciousness-correlated Born-statistics deviation — and **none of the three appears in this article's four**. Point the rebuttal at the tenet clause rather than at the local list.
  **Budget**: 1111 words of headroom (1888 of 3000 hard). Sequencing: this task and the P1 above touch adjacent text and both bear on the L67 rebuttal — run the P1 first, or run them in one pass.

  **What the tenet now says**: "under any *unconditioned aggregate* test the mechanism is *empirically indistinguishable from chance*… Preservation binds only that unconditioned marginal, however: a deviation *conditioned* on intention, task or subject would test the corridor itself" — with one coarse-grain instance already run null (Maier et al. 2018).
